Abstract: The annotation cost for remote sensing object detection is high, while existing active learning methods still face several challenges in object detection scenarios, including the coupling of localization and classification uncertainty, severe localization noise in the cold-start stage, and pseudo-diversity caused by high-recall candidate proposals. To address these issues, we propose a foundation-model-collaborative active learning and semi-automatic annotation framework for efficient construction of remote sensing object detection datasets. We build a dual-source mechanism consisting of a reference localization source (SA-source) based on UPN+SAM2 and a detector prediction source (OD-source), and further propose a Foundation-model-enhanced Dual-Source Uncertainty estimation to improve sample selection quality in the cold-start stage by jointly modeling localization consistency and classification confidence. Furthermore, we propose Object-Centric Diversity Sampling, which constructs object-level representations using DINOv2 features and SAM2 masks to improve sample coverage while suppressing pseudo-diversity. To address geometric noise in the semi-automatic annotation stage, we design Dual-Source Box Switching, which replaces noisy detector boxes with matched refined boxes from the SA-source, thereby reducing the manual burden of box refinement. Experiments on DIOR, HRSC2016, DOTAv2, and FAIR1M show that our method achieves superior or comparable results under most annotation budgets, with notably stronger cold-start sample efficiency in the low-budget regime.

</details>


### [70] [OFD-Net: Teacher-Free Reliable Semi-supervised Medical Image Segmentation with Orthogonal Feature Disentanglement Net of Foreground-Background](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16705)
*Shao-feng Jiang,Zhe-yang Jing,Qin Lu,Huan-huan Shi,Zhen Chen,Cong-xuan zhang,Chen Yi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OFD-Net是一个无教师单网络框架，通过正交特征解耦模块实现可靠的半监督医学图像分割，减少错误积累并缓解确认偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有半监督学习方法主要依赖伪标签生成，但缺乏明确的伪标签质量评估结构参考。低质量伪标签在处理外观变化大的未标记数据时可能导致不可靠训练、错误积累和确认偏差。

Method: 提出OFD-Net框架，包含：1）正交特征解耦模块（OFDM），将未标记数据解耦为背景和前景表示；2）解耦指导模块（DGM），通过可变形卷积将前景-背景结构先验注入解码器；3）可靠性感知伪标签学习机制，根据主预测与解耦响应的结构一致性评估监督可靠性。

Result: 在四个公开医学图像分割基准测试（ISIC-2016、Kvasir-SEG、Synapse、ACDC）上进行了广泛实验，验证了OFD-Net的有效性。

Conclusion: 正交前景-背景解耦使OFD-Net能够在无教师单网络框架中建立高效可靠的训练范式，有效减少错误积累并缓解确认偏差。

Abstract: Semi-supervised learning (SSL) is an effective solution for medical image segmentation with limited annotations. Existing SSL methods mainly rely on pseudo-labels generated by teacher-student supervision or cross-network consistency. However, these methods lack an explicit structural reference for judging pseudo-label quality. Low-quality pseudo-labels may lead to unreliable training, error accumulation and confirmation bias when processing unlabeled data with substantial appearance variations. To address this issue, we proposed OFD-Net, a teacher-free single-network framework for reliable semi-supervised medical image segmentation. OFD-Net employs an Orthogonal Feature Disentanglement Module (OFDM) to capture OFD features for reliable SSL by disentangling unlabeled data into background and foreground representations with a reliable structural distribution, thereby effectively reducing error accumulation and alleviating confirmation bias among unlabeled data. Specifically, OFD-Net explicitly employs a Disentanglement Guidance Module (DGM) to inject the resulting structural priors of foreground-background into the decoder by deformable convolution processing, and outputs predictions with clearer foreground representations. Based on DGM and the OFDM, we further develop a reliability-aware pseudo-label learning mechanism that evaluates unlabeled supervision according to the structural consistency between the main prediction and the disentangled foreground-background responses, and then down-weights unreliable regions during training. Extensive experiments on four public medical image segmentation benchmarks, namely ISIC-2016, Kvasir-SEG, Synapse, and ACDC, validate the effectiveness of OFD-Net. These results confirm that orthogonal foreground-background disentanglement enables OFD-Net to establish an efficient and reliable training paradigm within a teacher-free single-network framework.

</details>


### [71] [O-VAD: Industrial Video Anomaly Detection through Object-Centric Tracking and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18142)
*Mei Yuan,Qi Long,Qifeng Wu,Zhenyang Li,Yizhou Zhao,Lei Wang,Yang Liu,Min Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练的智能体框架，用于工业视频异常检测，通过追踪物体状态演变和时空动态来识别异常，优于现有方法且提供可解释报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于VLM的异常检测方法在通用领域表现良好，但在工业场景中性能下降，因为工业环境具有复杂的物体变换、严格的物理和程序约束等特征。

Method: 引入无需训练的智能体框架，无需领域特定知识，通过追踪检测对象的时空动态和底层变换，基于对象级时间状态轨迹进行异常推理。

Result: 在三个IVAD数据集上的实验表明，该方法优于前沿VLM、智能体框架和在相应数据集上微调的传统VAD方法，同时提供异常过程和类型的可解释报告。

Conclusion: 提出的训练自由智能体框架有效解决了工业视频异常检测的复杂性问题，通过类似人类检查员的物体状态演变分析，实现了高性能和可解释性的异常检测。

Abstract: Industrial Video Anomaly Detection (IVAD) aims to identify anomalous objects and events in an industrial process, which is crucial for modern manufacturing and quality control systems. Existing VLM-based anomaly reasoning methods are capable of detecting open-ended anomalies in general domains. However, their performance declines in industrial settings characterized by intricate object transformations, strict physics, and procedural constraints. To tackle the complexity of such interaction-intensive detection, we introduce a training-free agentic framework for anomaly detection free of domain-specific knowledge, emphasizing object state evolution like humans inspectors. It is designed to track spatial-temporal dynamics and underlying transformations of detected objects over time, and then reason over the object-wise temporal state trajectories to identify abnormal objects in grounded frames. Our method overcomes limitations of prior approaches that rely on retraining on normal clips or injecting domain knowledge as context for test-time inference. Extensive experiments on three IVAD datasets demonstrate that our method outperforms frontier VLMs, agentic frameworks, and traditional VAD methods fine-tuned on the respective datasets, while providing interpretable reports over anomaly processes and types.

</details>


### [72] [Can Experts Adapt Without Training? On Test-Time Modality Generalization in MVLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16726)
*Raza Imam,Darakshan Rashid,Yutong Xie,Dwarikanath Mahapatra,Brejesh Lall,Mohammad Yaqub*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需优化的测试时模态泛化框架MoBE，通过熵引导动态路由和贝叶斯自适应，显著提升了医学视觉语言模型在未见模态和领域的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学视觉语言模型在零样本泛化方面表现良好，但其可靠性在面对未见模态和领域时会急剧下降，而这恰恰是临床鲁棒性最重要的场景。现有方法在测试时存在专门化-泛化困境。

Method: 提出MoBE框架：基于混合专家思想，采用熵引导的动态路由机制结合专家级贝叶斯自适应，在测试时无需梯度更新即可实现专家的动态选择和自适应。该方法无需参数优化，通过测试时路由和在线统计增强静态MVLM。

Result: MoBE在已见、未见和异质医学基准测试中，分别取得了比最先进TTA方法平均高出+4.72、+7.17和+4.3的准确率提升，证明了训练自由专家自适应对于鲁棒模态泛化的有效性。

Conclusion: MoBE框架成功解决了医学视觉语言模型在测试时的模态泛化挑战，通过完全无优化的专家路由和自适应机制，显著提升了模型在临床关键场景下的鲁棒性和可靠性。

Abstract: Medical vision-language models (MVLMs) promise broad zero-shot generalization, yet their reliability collapses when confronted with unseen modalities and domains, precisely where clinical robustness matters most. To address this gap, we revisit test-time modality generalization from the perspective of Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) and ask: can experts route-and-adapt without any optimization during inference? We identify a fundamental specialization-generalization dilemma at test time, where blindly aggregating modality experts dilutes modality-specific knowledge, while selecting one highly confident expert risks mismatch under shift. To address this, we propose MoBE: a fully optimization-free framework that performs dynamic expert selection and adaptation at test time. MoBE combines entropy-guided dynamic routing in MoE settings with expert-wise Bayesian adaptation, enabling experts to update their confidence and adapt online without gradient updates. Without parametric updates, MoBE augments a static MVLM with test-time routing and online statistics, achieving average accuracy gains of +4.72, +7.17, and +4.3 over state-of-the-art TTA methods across seen, unseen, and heterogeneous medical benchmarks, highlighting the effectiveness of training-free expert adaptation for robust modality generalization.

</details>


### [73] [Multi-Dimensional Quality Assessment for AI-Generated Human-Centric Videos: Dataset and Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16742)
*Sijing Wu,Yunhao Li,Huiyu Duan,Yucheng Zhu,Xiongkuo Min,Patrick Le Callet,Guangtao Zhai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了HVEval+数据集和MoE-Rater方法，用于AI生成以人为中心视频的质量评估。HVEval+是目前最大的该类视频质量评估数据集，包含1k提示、20k视频和大量人工标注。MoE-Rater是基于MLLM的一体化多任务方法，支持多维度质量评分、比较和问答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成以人为中心的视频在现代应用中至关重要，但常存在质量问题和语义不匹配。现有评估方法不足，需要有效的质量评估方案来促进T2V模型的优化和发展。

Method: 1. 构建HVEval+数据集：在原有HVEval基础上增加成对偏好标注，包含1k提示、20k视频、60k MOS分数、60k偏好对和20k问答对，覆盖空间质量、时间质量和文本-视频对应三个维度。
2. 提出MoE-Rater方法：基于混合专家和多模态大语言模型的一体化方法，支持多任务。引入MoPE（混合投影器专家）和MoLE（混合LoRA专家），采用三阶段训练策略：任务感知预训练、任务特定适应和自适应路由优化。

Result: 1. HVEval+是目前最大的AI生成以人为中心视频质量评估数据集，提供了全面的评估基准。
2. MoE-Rater在HVEval+和Human-AGVQA数据集上表现出优越性能，展示了统一多任务的能力。
3. 实验和分析表明该方法在视频质量评估和T2V模型优化方面具有显著潜力。

Conclusion: HVEval+数据集和MoE-Rater方法为AI生成视频质量评估提供了重要工具，能有效评估多维度质量并支持多任务统一处理，有助于推动T2V模型的评估和优化发展。

Abstract: AI-generated human-centric videos play a crucial role in a wide range of modern applications. However, they often suffer from quality issues and semantic mismatches, underscoring the importance of effective quality assessment for such videos. To this end, we extend our previous dataset HVEval with pairwise preference annotations, resulting in HVEval+, the largest holistic quality assessment dataset for AI-generated human-centric videos, which comprises 1k prompts based on a comprehensive taxonomy, 20k videos generated by 24 text-to-video (T2V) models, and extensive human annotations, including 60k mean opinion scores (MOSs) and 60k preference pairs across 3 dimensions (i.e., spatial quality, temporal quality, and text-video correspondence), as well as 20k category-specific question-answer (Q&A) pairs. Along with the HVEval+ dataset, we further propose MoE-Rater, a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE)-inspired and multimodal large language model (MLLM)-based all-in-one method that supports multi-dimensional quality rating, multi-dimensional pairwise comparison, and category-specific question answering within a single model. Specifically, we introduce Mixture of Projector Experts (MoPE) and Mixture of LoRA Experts (MoLE), together with a three-stage training strategy consisting of task-aware pre-training, task-specific adaptation, and adaptive routing optimization, to effectively unify multiple tasks, resulting in superior performance on both HVEval+ and Human-AGVQA datasets. Extensive experiments and comprehensive analysis demonstrate the significant potential of the HVEval+ dataset and the MoE-Rater method in advancing AI-generated video quality assessment and further facilitating the evaluation and optimization of T2V models.

</details>


### [74] [VisionAssist: An Open-Source Smartphone Assistant for AI-Based Visual Accessibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16750)
*Ayşe Özlem Çalışkan,Jordi Sanchez-Riera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VisionAssist是一款开源移动应用程序，通过人工智能视觉辅助功能帮助低视力人群提高独立性，集成了物体定位、图像描述和紧急联系人/日历提醒三大功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低视力人群在日常生活中面临视觉信息解读的挑战，需要辅助技术来提高独立性和生活质量。现有的解决方案可能分散或不完整，需要整合多种功能于统一平台。

Method: 开发了开源移动应用程序VisionAssist，集成了三大功能：1) 通过分析实时摄像头流定位特定物体；2) 生成捕获图像的语音描述；3) 整合手机联系人和日历以支持紧急呼叫和语音提醒。采用语音命令进行免手操作，并通过文本转语音提供反馈。

Result: 成功开发了一个完全可访问的移动应用程序，将多种辅助服务整合到统一平台中。应用程序支持免手交互，通过语音命令和文本转语音反馈，源代码已公开在GitHub上供社区贡献。

Conclusion: VisionAssist通过整合多种AI驱动的视觉辅助功能于单一开源平台，提高了低视力用户的独立性。开源发布旨在鼓励社区贡献，加速无障碍技术发展，为解决低视力人群面临的日常挑战提供了实用的技术解决方案。

Abstract: People with low vision often face challenges in performing everyday tasks that require interpreting visual information. We present \textbf{VisionAssist}, an open-source mobile application designed to improve independence by providing AI-powered visual assistance through a smartphone. The application integrates three complementary functionalities within a single interface. First, it enables users to locate specific objects by analyzing the live camera feed. Second, it generates spoken descriptions of captured images, allowing users to identify visual content such as food labels, documents, and everyday objects. Third, it integrates with the smartphone's contacts and calendar to facilitate emergency calls and provide voice-based reminders. The application supports hands-free interaction through voice commands and delivers all feedback using text-to-speech synthesis, making it fully accessible to users with visual impairments. By combining multiple assistive services into a unified platform and releasing the project as open-source software, the proposed solution aims to encourage community contributions and accelerate the development of accessible technologies. The source code is publicly available at: https://github.com/AOzlemC/LowVisionProject.git

</details>


### [75] [Hybrid Machine Learning for Articulation Angle Estimation of Truck-Semitrailer Combinations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16758)
*Qixuan Zhang,Jonas Boettcher,Simon F. G. Ehlers,Marvin Stuede*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多种基于学习的模型，直接从视觉和运动学输入估计铰接角，无需专门初始化操作、边界框标注、拖车传感器信号或拖车参数先验知识，通过EKF框架和自适应加权方案在实际场景中实现准确可靠的铰接角估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卡车拖车铰接角的准确估计对自动驾驶和ADAS至关重要，但现有方法需要手动初始化、额外传感器、拖车先验知识或缺乏实际验证，限制了实际部署。

Method: 提出多种基于学习的模型直接从视觉和运动学输入估计铰接角；将两个学习模型与运动学模型集成在扩展卡尔曼滤波器框架中；对涉及视觉输入的测量应用基于不确定性量化的自适应加权方案。

Result: 通过不同拖车类型的广泛实际实验，方法在域外条件下（包括新拖车、不同颜色和光照条件）展现了鲁棒性和泛化能力；混合方法实现了准确可靠的铰接角估计，同时减少了实现需求并具备实际部署优势。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够准确可靠地估计铰接角，消除了对专门初始化、额外传感器、拖车先验知识的依赖，在实际部署中具有优势，展示了在自动驾驶和ADAS应用中的实用价值。

Abstract: Accurate articulation angle estimation of trucks with trailers is critical for autonomous driving and advanced driver assistance system (ADAS). Existing methods either require manual initialization, additional sensors, or prior knowledge and signals from trailers, or they lack real-world validation, limiting practical deployment. This paper presents multiple learning-based models to directly estimate articulation angles from visual and kinematic inputs, eliminating the need for dedicated driving maneuvers for initialization, bounding box annotations, trailer-mounted sensor signals, or prior knowledge of trailer parameters. Two learning-based models are integrated with a kinematic model within an extended Kalman filter (EKF) framework, and an adaptive weighting scheme based on uncertainty quantification is applied for measurements involving visual input. Extensive real-world experiments with different trailer types demonstrate the approaches' robustness and generalization under out-of-domain conditions, including new trailers, varying colors, and lighting conditions. Results show that the hybrid method achieves accurate and reliable articulation angle estimation while maintaining reduced implementation requirements and practical deployment advantages.

</details>


### [76] [Spatiotemporal Facial Action Unit Detection using Twin Cycle Autoencoders for Driver Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16760)
*Sai Sidharth D*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Twin Cycle Autoencoder (TCA)用于驾驶员监控中的面部动作单元检测，通过耦合的空间和时间周期自编码器分支处理光照变化、遮挡和短暂AU激活等挑战，在基准数据集和实际驾驶场景中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶员监控系统需要可靠的面部动作单元检测来推断疲劳、分心和认知负荷。然而，驾驶环境中的光照变化、部分遮挡、头部姿态变化以及AU激活的微妙性和短暂性给现有方法带来挑战。现有AU检测器通常将空间外观和时间动态分开处理，无法充分利用大量未标记驾驶视频中的自监督信号。

Method: TCA架构包含两个耦合的周期一致自编码器分支：1) 空间周期自编码器，通过图像级周期一致性从身份特征中解耦AU相关外观；2) 时间周期自编码器，在潜在AU轨迹上实施前向-后向一致性以捕捉onset-apex-offset动态。两个分支通过跨分支潜在对齐损失耦合，并通过注意力模块融合后进行多标签AU分类。

Result: 在DISFA和BP4D基准测试以及自然驾驶数据集上评估，TCA相比CNN-RNN、3D-CNN和图基AU基线方法表现更优，特别是在与疲劳相关的低强度和快速转换AU上（如AU45、AU43和打哈欠相关的AU26）。模型在嵌入式Jetson Xavier NX平台上支持实时处理。

Conclusion: TCA通过空间和时间周期一致性学习的耦合架构有效提升了驾驶场景中面部动作单元的检测性能，特别是对低强度和快速变化的AU，为生产级高级驾驶辅助系统提供了可行的实时解决方案。

Abstract: Driver monitoring systems (DMS) increasingly rely on facial cues to infer drowsiness, distraction, and cognitive load in real time. Facial Action Units (AUs), grounded in the Facial Action Coding System (FACS), provide an objective and interpretable representation of such states, but their automatic detection in the driving context is complicated by low and variable illumination, partial occlusion, head-pose variation, and the subtlety and short duration of relevant AU activations. Existing AU detectors largely treat spatial appearance and temporal dynamics separately, limiting their ability to exploit self-supervisory signal from abundant unlabeled driving video. We propose the Twin Cycle Autoencoder (TCA), a spatiotemporal architecture composed of two coupled cycle-consistent autoencoder branches: a Spatial Cycle Autoencoder that disentangles AU-relevant appearance from identity through image-level cycle consistency, and a Temporal Cycle Autoencoder that enforces forward-backward consistency over latent AU trajectories to capture onset-apex-offset dynamics. The two branches are coupled through a cross-branch latent alignment loss and fused via an attention module before multi-label AU classification. We evaluate TCA on the DISFA and BP4D benchmarks and on an in-cabin naturalistic driving dataset, and observe consistent improvements over CNN-RNN, 3D-CNN, and graph-based AU baselines, particularly for low-intensity and rapidly transitioning AUs relevant to fatigue (AU45, AU43) and yawning (AU26). We further show the model sustains real-time throughput on an embedded Jetson Xavier NX platform, supporting its use in production-grade advanced driver assistance systems (ADAS).

</details>


### [77] [HTT-Net: Hierarchical Text-guided Transition Modeling for Surgical Video Phase Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16787)
*Kunjie Deng,Jinghui Zhang,Weidong Chen,Ganbin Li,Xiangjun Lyu,Zhendong Mao,Yingchi Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HTT-Net是一个用于手术视频阶段识别的分层文本引导过渡建模网络，通过引入结构化手术语义知识来改进阶段感知片段构建和语义细化，解决局部视觉模糊性和瞬态预测噪声问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术视频阶段识别在计算机辅助干预中至关重要，用于工作流理解、术中引导和手术质量评估。现有方法面临局部视觉模糊性、瞬态预测噪声以及对程序语义利用不足的挑战，导致识别准确性和时间一致性受限。

Method: 提出HTT-Net网络，核心是引入结构化手术语义知识：1)构建分层手术语义记忆库，包含阶段内描述、阶段间过渡描述和细粒度语义单元；2)提出过渡感知片段构建(TAS-Con)，将帧级证据组织成连贯的片段表示，并利用阶段间过渡描述处理边界片段；3)提出过渡感知片段校准(TAS-Calib)，通过分层手术语义校准阶段感知片段表示，在视觉模糊情况下提高判别能力。

Result: 在Cholec80和LCRS-100数据集上的实验证明了HTT-Net在稳健手术视频阶段识别方面的有效性。

Conclusion: HTT-Net通过分层文本引导过渡建模有效解决了手术视频阶段识别中的视觉模糊性和瞬态噪声问题，引入的结构化手术语义知识显著提升了识别的准确性和时间一致性，为手术工作流理解提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Surgical video phase recognition is a fundamental task in computer-assisted intervention, supporting workflow understanding, intraoperative guidance, and surgical quality assessment. Although recent visual-temporal models have achieved promising progress, accurate and temporally coherent phase recognition remains challenging due to local visual ambiguity, transient prediction noise, and insufficient use of procedural semantics. To address these challenges, we propose HTT-Net, a Hierarchical Text-guided Transition modeling Network for surgical video phase recognition. The key idea is to introduce structured surgical semantic knowledge into phase-aware segment construction and semantic refinement. Specifically, we construct a hierarchical surgical semantic memory with intra-phase descriptions, inter-phase transition descriptions, and fine-grained semantic units. Based on this memory, the proposed Transition-Aware Segment Construction (TAS-Con) organizes frame-level evidence into coherent segment representations and handles boundary clips with inter-phase transition descriptions. Furthermore, we introduce Transition-Aware Segment Calibration (TAS-Calib), which calibrates phase-aware segment representations through hierarchical surgical semantics and improves discrimination under visual ambiguity without dense frame-level vision-language fusion. Experiments on Cholec80 and LCRS-100 demonstrate the effectiveness of HTT-Net for robust surgical video phase recognition.

</details>


### [78] [Scene-SAM3D: Multi-View Scene Asset Generation Without Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16805)
*Yuqi Zhang,Yadan Luo,Xiangyu Sun,Fengyi Zhang,Zi Huang,Xin Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Scene-SAM3D是一个无需训练的框架，将SAM3D从单视图物体生成扩展到校准多视图场景资产生成，通过互补视图选择、潜在速度融合和轻量级刚性物体高斯优化，显著提升了场景重建质量和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管SAM3D等单图像3D生成模型具有强大的物体先验，但在真实世界场景中，严重的遮挡、冗余观察和跨视图不一致使得可靠的场景生成具有挑战性。高质量3D场景资产对于机器人操作、导航和仿真等具体应用至关重要。

Method: 1) 选择一组紧凑的互补视图，减少观察冗余并为遮挡区域提供额外证据；2) 基于选定视图执行步高效潜在速度融合，集成多视图证据并抑制规范空间中的跨视图冲突；3) 采用轻量级刚性物体高斯优化，在200次迭代内细化场景布局，同时保持生成的物体几何形状。

Result: 在Replica和ScanNet++数据集上的实验表明，该方法在实例和场景级别均取得一致改进：在Replica上将场景级CD降低了43.8%，在ScanNet++上降低了30.9%，同时在同一多视图设置下将流模型采样FLOPs和wall-time延迟降低了近20%。

Conclusion: Scene-SAM3D成功将SAM3D扩展到多视图场景生成，通过训练免费的框架显著提高了3D场景重建的质量和效率，为具体应用提供了高质量的3D场景资产。

Abstract: High-quality 3D scene assets are critical for embodied applications such as robotic manipulation, navigation, and simulation. Despite their strong object priors, recent single-image 3D generation models such as SAM3D remain insufficient for real-world scenes, where severe occlusions, redundant observations, and cross-view inconsistencies make reliable scene generation challenging. We introduce Scene-SAM3D, a training-free framework that extends SAM3D from single-view object generation to calibrated multi-view scene asset generation. Scene-SAM3D selects a compact set of complementary views, reducing observation redundancy while providing additional evidence for regions occluded in individual views. Based on the selected views, it performs step-efficient latent velocity fusion to integrate multi-view evidence and suppress cross-view conflicts in canonical space. Finally, a lightweight rigid-object Gaussian optimization refines the scene layout within 200 iterations while preserving the generated object geometry. Experiments on Replica and ScanNet++ demonstrate consistent improvements at both instance and scene levels, with our method reducing scene-level CD by 43.8% on Replica and 30.9% on ScanNet++, while cutting flow-model sampling FLOPs and wall-time latency by nearly 20% under the same multi-view setting. Code will be released at https://github.com/xibi777/Scene-SAM3D.

</details>


### [79] [Test-Time Registers as Global Priors for Tokenized Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16824)
*Cheng-Yao Hong,Yifan Wang,Yuewei Lin,Chenyu You*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究者发现视觉Transformer中的register tokens（注意力汇聚点）具有低频结构特征，提出RegToken方法将其转化为可插拔的全局先验令牌，从而在不重训练的情况下提升tokenized图像生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作主要通过可解释性分析和线性探针研究register tokens，但未探究其是否能作为即插即用的生成信号而无需重新训练。在tokenized图像生成中，研究者希望利用register的低频结构特征来改进生成效果。

Method: 1) 使用OpenCLIP和DINOv2在ImageNet上分析register特征的低频特性；2) 提出RegToken训练自由方法：通过NFN-based层定位、TokenRank引导的子空间提取以及register子空间上的投影-守恒更新，将register结构转化为少量全局先验令牌；3) 将RegToken插入冻结的1D token生成流水线中。

Result: 1) register特征相比[CLS]读数和patch均值特征表现出更强的低频集中性，与像素空间DCT低频能量有中等程度的相关性；2) RegToken在不修改预训练权重的情况下，改善了ImageNet生成和对齐指标（如FID-5k从20.5降到20.1，SigLIP从3.6提升到3.9）；3) 加速了测试时优化（Steps@τ从74减少到52）。

Conclusion: 通常被视为注意力伪影的register结构可以被重新用作tokenized生成的轻量级全局先验，展示了注意力汇聚点的实用价值，为训练自由的生成改进提供了新思路。

Abstract: Attention-based models often develop attention sinks, where a small number of tokens repeatedly attract attention and accumulate unusually large activations. In vision transformers, these outliers are closely related to registers, which have been diagnostically linked to global, low-frequency image structure. Existing work has largely studied registers through interpretability analyses and linear probes, leaving open whether they can be operationalized as plug-and-play signals for generation without retraining. We revisit this question in tokenized image generation. Using OpenCLIP and DINOv2 on ImageNet, we find that test-time register features exhibit stronger low-frequency concentration than both [CLS] readouts and patch-mean features, and show a consistent (albeit moderate) correlation with pixel-space DCT low-frequency energy. Motivated by these diagnostics, we introduce RegToken, a training-free procedure that converts register structure into a small set of global prior tokens by (i) NFN-based layer localization, (ii) TokenRank-guided subspace extraction, and (iii) a projection-and-conservation update on the register subspace. Inserted into a frozen compact 1D token generation pipeline, RegToken improves ImageNet generation and alignment metrics (e.g., FID-5k 20.5 to 20.1, SigLIP 3.6 to 3.9) without modifying pretrained weights, and accelerates test-time optimization (Steps@$τ$ 74 to 52). Overall, our results suggest that structures often viewed as attention artifacts can be repurposed as lightweight global priors for tokenized generation.

</details>


### [80] [Spatially-Aware Class-Agnostic Object Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16826)
*Robert Wijaya,Md. Tanvir Hossain,Amanda Kau,Ngai-Man Cheung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UpCount是一种类无关的目标计数方法，通过增强视觉表示和改进空间结构建模来准确估计任意类别目标的数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有广义目标计数方法在结构复杂的目标上表现不佳，主要受限于空间建模能力不足。需要更好的方法来保留空间结构信息，提高对任意目标类别的计数准确性。

Method: 使用ViT-B/16编码器提取多层特征，通过Dense Prediction Transformers和FeatUp重新组装成细化的多尺度金字塔特征，增强结构和空间敏感性；采用提议-验证计数头识别重复模式并生成密度图进行最终计数。

Result: 在FSC-147数据集上达到12.39 MAE和100.89 RMSE测试性能，在CARPK车辆计数数据集上表现出良好的迁移能力，达到6.27 MAE和8.79 RMSE。

Conclusion: UpCount通过增强视觉表示和改进空间结构建模，有效解决了广义目标计数中结构复杂目标的挑战，在标准数据集上取得了竞争性性能，并展示了良好的跨数据集迁移能力。

Abstract: Generalised object counting aims to estimate the number of instances of an arbitrary object category from a single image, but many recent methods can struggle on structurally complex objects due to limited spatial modelling. We present \textit{UpCount}, a class-agnostic counter designed to better preserve spatial structure. UpCount strengthens the visual representation by extracting multi-layer features from a ViT-B/16 encoder and reassembling them into a refined multi-scale pyramid that is spatially refined using Dense Prediction Transformers and FeatUp, yielding features with improved structural and spatial sensitivity; a proposal--verification counting head then identifies repeated patterns and produces a density map for the final count. On FSC-147, UpCount achieves 12.39 MAE and 100.89 RMSE on the test set, and it transfers effectively to vehicle counting on CARPK (6.27 MAE, 8.79 RMSE). Code: https://github.com/r28112072-rgb/upcount

</details>


### [81] [UniNDM: A Unified Noise-driven Detection and Mitigation Framework Against Sexual Content in Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16828)
*Yao Huang,Yitong Sun,Huanran Chen,Ruochen Zhang,Shouwei Ruan,Ranjie Duan,Maoxun Yuan,Yinpeng Dong,Hui Xue,Xiaochun Cao,Xingxing Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniNDM是一个统一的噪声驱动框架，通过分析扩散过程中的噪声动力学来防御文本到图像扩散模型中隐式色情提示的攻击，提供检测和缓解双重保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像扩散模型对隐式色情提示存在脆弱性，这些提示通过伪装成良性术语或对抗性令牌来意外生成不当内容。现有安全机制存在根本局限性：检测方法主要识别显式内容，无法捕捉隐式恶意意图；缓解方法依赖于静态负面提示，无法适应多样化的隐式场景。

Method: 基于扩散过程中噪声动力学的洞察：早期预测噪声在正常内容和色情内容之间具有固有可分性，且语义集中度随时间步长二次增加。开发轻量级噪声检测器（无计算开销），并引入噪声增强的自适应负面引导：通过大语言模型动态生成上下文特定的负面提示来处理多样化隐式内容，同时通过抑制对显式令牌的注意力集中来优化初始噪声。此外，将框架扩展到扩散变换器架构，通过区域约束语义引导适配其统一的多模态注意力机制。

Result: 在U-Net模型和DiT模型上的综合实验，包括自然和对抗性数据集，显示相对于最先进方法（如SLD、UCE、Safree等）有显著改进。

Conclusion: UniNDM通过重新思考扩散过程中的噪声动力学，提供了一种统一且有效的框架来防御文本到图像扩散模型中的隐式色情提示，展示了在检测准确性和计算效率方面的优越性能，并为未来扩散模型安全研究提供了新视角。

Abstract: Despite the impressive generative capabilities of text-to-image diffusion models, they remain vulnerable to implicit sexual prompts, where subtle cues disguised as benign terms or adversarial tokens unexpectedly generate the inappropriate content due to model biases or latent correlations in training data. Existing safety mechanisms face fundamental limitations: detection methods primarily identify explicit content and fail to capture implicit malicious intent, while mitigation approaches rely on static negative prompts inadequate for diverse implicit scenarios. To address these challenges, we propose UniNDM, a unified noise-driven framework that rethinks safety mechanisms through the lens of noise dynamics in diffusion processes. Our key insight is that early-stage predicted noise exhibits inherent separability between normal and sexually explicit content, which we theoretically demonstrates quadratically increasing semantic concentration with timestep. Leveraging this property, we develop a lightweight noise-based detector achieving superior accuracy with virtually no computational overhead. For mitigation, we introduce noise-enhanced adaptive negative guidance: dynamically generating context-specific negative prompts via large language models to handle diverse implicit content, while optimizing initial noise by suppressing attention concentration on explicit tokens to provide comprehensive protection. Besides the U-Net-based diffusion models, we further extend our framework to emerging Diffusion Transformer architectures through region-constrained semantic guidance tailored for their unified multimodal attention. Comprehensive experiments across U-Net models and DiT models on both natural and adversarial datasets demonstrate substantial improvements over state-of-the-art methods, including SLD, UCE, Safree, etc. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Aries-iai/UniNDM.

</details>


### [82] [Robust PnP on a Neuromorphic Processor for Object Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16834)
*Tam Ngoc-Bang Nguyen,Mohsi Jawaid,Tat-Jun Chin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种神经形态可部署的鲁棒PnP解决方案，结合脉冲神经网络预测2D标志点和分布式算法进行刚体姿态估计，在神经形态硬件上实现了高能效的物体姿态估计流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经形态计算在机器人感知中具有更高能效，但现有方法主要关注神经网络任务，对于非学习任务（如PnP优化）的神经形态解决方案较少，这限制了神经形态计算在需要学习和非学习组件的完整感知流程中的应用。

Method: 1) 提出神经形态可部署的鲁棒PnP公式，通过分布式算法在神经形态处理器上执行刚体姿态的鲁棒最小二乘估计；2) 设计脉冲神经网络(SNN)从事件数据预测2D标志点，主要层按照脉冲神经元原理设计。

Result: 在Intel Loihi 2神经形态硬件上的结果表明，神经形态鲁棒PnP实现了更高的能量效率，同时保持了竞争性的准确性。

Conclusion: 该工作实现了物体姿态估计流程的主要阶段的神经形态处理，包括事件感知、学习标志点预测和几何优化，为神经形态计算在完整感知流程中的应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Neuromorphic computing is gaining attention in robotic perception due to its higher energy efficiency. While neural network-based methods can more readily exploit the distributed and parallelized structure of neuromorphic computers, crafting neuromorphic solutions for non-learning tasks is less straightforward. This hampers the usage of neuromorphic computing for perception pipelines that depend on both learning and non-learning components, such as object pose estimation (OPE) where state-of-the-art methods use a deep network to predict 2D landmarks and nonlinear optimization to solve perspective-n-point (PnP). In this paper, we propose a novel neuromorphic-deployable formulation for robust PnP, where given outlier-prone 2D-3D correspondences, the object pose with the largest number of inliers is determined. Underpinning our method is a distributed algorithm for robust least squares estimation of rigid body pose that can be executed on a neuromorphic processor. We also design a spiking neural network (SNN) to predict 2D landmarks from event data, where the main layers of the SNN were designed according to the principles of spiking neurons. Overall, our work enables neuromorphic treatment of the major stages of an OPE pipeline, from event sensing and learned landmark prediction, to geometric optimization for robust PnP. Results on neuromophic hardware (Intel Loihi 2) indicate the higher energy efficiency our neuromorphic robust PnP, while achieving competitive accuracy.

</details>


### [83] [TopoGS: Planar Reconstruction via Topology-aware 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16838)
*Shanshan Pan,Jiale Chen,Yilin Liu,Hui Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TopoGS：首个整合平面和拓扑约束的3D高斯泼溅框架，通过从多视图图像分割中提取全局2D拓扑关系并将高斯基元锚定到这些结构元素，实现连贯的3D重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D高斯泼溅方法虽然集成了平面基元以产生紧凑可编辑的几何体，但通常将平面视为孤立的离散集合。缺乏拓扑连接性阻碍了稳健的几何推理，导致碎片化重建和对齐不当的边界，无法满足严格空间分析和专业设计流程的精度要求。

Method: 从多视图图像分割中提取全局2D拓扑关系，将高斯基元锚定到这些结构元素上，共同优化平面参数、渲染保真度和拓扑邻接关系，并强制执行严格的多视图一致性约束。

Result: 在ScanNet++数据集上的广泛评估表明，TopoGS达到了最先进的性能，显著减轻了几何对齐错误，生成了连接良好、结构化的3D模型，提供了生成精确、拓扑合理且视觉保真的场景表示的稳健解决方案。

Conclusion: TopoGS通过显式整合平面和拓扑约束，实现了连贯的3D重建，解决了现有方法中的拓扑连接性缺失问题，为生成准确、拓扑合理且视觉保真的场景表示提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Extracting structured, parametric 3D representations from raw images remains a fundamental challenge in computer vision and graphics. While recent advancements in the 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) pipeline integrate planar primitives to yield compact and editable geometry, these approaches typically treat planes as isolated, discrete sets. This lack of topological connectivity hinders robust geometric reasoning, leading to fragmented reconstructions and misaligned boundaries that fall short of the precision for rigorous spatial analysis and professional design workflows. To address this, we introduce TopoGS, the first 3DGS framework to explicitly integrate both planar and topological constraints for coherent 3D reconstruction. Specifically, we extract global 2D topological relationships from multi-view image segmentations and anchor Gaussian primitives to these structural elements. This formulation enables the joint optimization of plane parameters, rendering fidelity, and topological adjacency. By enforcing strict multi-view consistency alongside these topological constraints, our method significantly mitigates geometric misalignments and produces connected, structured 3D models. Extensive evaluations on the ScanNet++ dataset demonstrate that TopoGS achieves state-of-the-art performance, providing a highly robust solution for generating accurate, topologically sound, and visually faithful scene representations.

</details>


### [84] [Look Clearly Before Answering: Mitigating Hallucinations in LVLMs via Saliency-Driven Perceptual Realignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16841)
*Pengxu Chen,Yao Zhu,Guangming Zhu,Jun Sheng,Jincai Huang,Xiangyang Ji,Liang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SDPR是一种无需训练的方法，通过显著性驱动的感知重校准来缓解大视觉语言模型中的幻觉问题，在多个基准测试中表现优异且计算开销低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大视觉语言模型在多模态理解方面表现出色，但仍容易产生与视觉证据不一致的幻觉响应。现有方法主要关注语言先验偏置或跨模态不平衡，而忽略了感知和记忆中的渐进性视觉退化问题。

Method: 提出了Saliency-Driven Perceptual Realignment (SDPR)框架，包含三个核心组件：1）显著性驱动的注意力重新分配，释放被非语义汇标记劫持的注意力；2）KV缓存空间失真识别及显著性驱动的缓存对齐，保护生成过程中的查询相关视觉特征；3）先验约束的对比解码，惩罚由主导语言先验引起的不忠实预测。

Result: 在多种LVLM架构上的广泛实验表明，SDPR在幻觉基准和通用基准上都优于最先进的方法，无需额外训练且运行时开销最小。

Conclusion: SDPR通过在整个生成轨迹中对视觉感知进行整体对齐，有效抵抗幻觉，为LVLM的幻觉缓解提供了鲁棒且高效的无训练解决方案。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in multimodal understanding. However, they remain prone to hallucinations, generating responses that are inconsistent with the visual evidence. Existing mitigation methods largely address language-prior bias or cross-modal imbalance, while progressive visual degradation across perception and memory remains underexplored. In this work, we propose Saliency-Driven Perceptual Realignment (SDPR), a training-free framework that mitigates the degradation of visual awareness throughout inference. Specifically, we first introduce saliency-driven attention redistribution to release attention hijacked by non-semantic sink tokens, thereby recovering critical visual evidence. Second, we identify spatial distortion in the KV cache and propose saliency-driven cache alignment to preserve query-relevant visual features during generation. Finally, we introduce prior-constrained contrastive decoding to penalize unfaithful predictions induced by dominant language priors. Our proposed SDPR is robust against hallucinations due to its holistic alignment of visual awareness across the entire generative trajectory. Extensive experiments across diverse LVLM architectures show that SDPR outperforms state-of-the-art methods on both hallucination and general-purpose benchmarks, requiring no additional training and incurring minimal runtime overhead. The code is available \href{https://github.com/PengSyuChen/SDPR}{\color{blue}{here}}.

</details>


### [85] [Dataset Distillation by Influence Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16859)
*Haoru Tan,Wang Wang,Sitong Wu,Xiuzhe Wu,Yangtian Sun,Chirui Chang,Shaofeng Zhang,Xiaojuan Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Inf-Match是一种新颖的数据集蒸馏方法，通过匹配最终训练结果而非训练过程，使用可微分的影响估计器对齐合成数据集和真实数据集对模型参数的影响，在分类和视觉语言任务上取得了最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集蒸馏方法主要关注对齐训练过程（如每步梯度或训练轨迹），但这些过程对齐方法存在启发式模仿的局限性。作者认为应该从结果中心视角重新审视数据集蒸馏，关注最终训练结果的对齐。

Method: 提出Influence Matching (Inf-Match)方法：1) 引入完全可微分、样本级别的影响估计器，通过展开优化动态和应用一阶泰勒近似，在线性时间内量化添加或移除数据对参数的影响；2) 通过最小化合成数据集与真实数据集影响的差异来学习合成集，实现结果对齐而非过程模仿。

Result: 在标准分类基准测试中取得最佳准确率：Tiny-ImageNet (IPC=10)达到31.5%，比NCFM提高4.7%。在视觉语言蒸馏方面，在Flickr30K上使用200-1000个合成样本，在图像/文本检索任务上取得领先表现，比NCFM高2.5%。

Conclusion: Inf-Match证明了从结果中心视角进行数据集蒸馏的有效性，通过匹配最终训练结果而非过程，提供了更直接的优化目标，在多个任务上超越了现有基于过程对齐的方法，为数据集蒸馏开辟了新方向。

Abstract: We revisit dataset distillation from an outcome-centric perspective. Rather than aligning process surrogates (per-step gradients or training trajectories), Influence Matching (Inf-Match) aligns the final outcome of training: it learns a compact synthetic set whose effect on the converged parameters matches that of the full dataset. Concretely, we introduce a fully differentiable, sample-level influence estimator that quantifies parameter shifts from adding or removing data, without time-consuming inverse-Hessian products or convexity assumptions. The estimator runs in linear time by unrolling the optimization dynamics and applying a first-order Taylor approximation. We then learn the synthetic set by minimizing the mismatch between its influence and that of the real dataset, yielding outcome alignment rather than heuristic process imitation. Inf-Match delivers the best accuracy across standard classification benchmarks. For instance, on Tiny-ImageNet (IPC=10), Inf-Match attains 31.5\%, a +4.7\% improvement over NCFM. Beyond classification, Inf-Match scales to vision-language distillation on Flickr30K, outperforming strong process-matching baselines. For instance, with 200 to 1000 synthetic samples, our method achieved a leading impressive average on image/text retrieval tasks, higher than NCFM by 2.5\%. The code will be released via https://github.com/hrtan/infmatch.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [86] [Multi-level context Modeling for consistent expert selection in Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16427)
*Shuhan Huang,Naifan Zhang,Yuanbo Tang,Yang Li,Wai Kin Victor Chan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MCF-MOE：一种通过多层上下文融合构建上下文感知表示，以解决MoE中因上下文不完整导致的专家选择不稳定问题的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE路由器基于浅层或孤立令牌表示进行专家选择，导致跨层路由决策不稳定且语义不一致。关键瓶颈在于上下文不完整性限制了专家的有效专业化。

Method: 提出MCF-MOE框架，通过整合跨层语义聚合和局部令牌级交互的互补信号，构建上下文感知表示，实现更信息丰富且一致的专家选择。

Result: 在语言建模和理解基准测试中，MCF-MOE持续提高路由一致性和下游性能，优于现有的MoE基线模型。

Conclusion: 上下文完整性对于专家路由至关重要，MCF-MOE通过多层上下文融合有效解决了现有MoE路由器的局限性，提升了路由质量和模型性能。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) enables efficient scaling of Transformer models by routing tokens to a small subset of experts. However, existing routers typically condition expert selection on shallow or isolated token representations, which often produce unstable and semantically inconsistent routing decisions across layers. In this work, we revisit expert selection from a representation perspective and identify context incompleteness as a key bottleneck limiting effective expert specialization. To address this issue, we propose Multi-level Context Fusion MOE (MCF-MOE), a framework that constructs context-aware representations by integrating complementary signals from cross-layer semantic aggregation and local token-level interactions, enabling more informative and consistent expert selection. Experiments on language modeling and understanding benchmarks demonstrate that MCF-MOE consistently improves routing consistency and downstream performance over strong MoE baselines, highlighting the importance of contextual completeness in expert routing. The code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/MCFMOE.

</details>


### [87] [RIMS: Preference Optimization via Smoothed Multi-pair Aggregation for Small-Scale LLM Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16431)
*Pei Tian,Zihan Dong,Tianci Liu,Linjun Zhang,Haoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RIMS是一个三阶段的偏好优化框架，用于提升小型语言模型在检索增强生成任务中对噪声的鲁棒性，通过软聚合机制替代硬选择，充分利用所有偏好对信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型语言模型在资源受限场景的检索增强生成中很吸引人，但容量有限使其对噪声检索证据高度敏感。现有基于偏好的方法（如RoseRAG）要么通过硬argmin/argmax选择最难的单个偏好对而丢弃其余信号，要么将多个偏好对视为独立二值比较导致数据利用率低。

Method: 提出RIMS框架，包含三个阶段：1）使用目标小型语言模型自身（不依赖专有模型）通过拒绝采样生成合成思维链偏好数据；2）可微分软聚合机制，用平滑算子替代硬选择，保留所有偏好对的梯度信号，同时保持边缘感知选择的判别结构；3）将平滑目标应用于多种对齐算法的偏好优化。

Result: 在四个多跳问答基准测试中，RIMS在多个小型语言模型骨干上都优于最先进的基线方法，在噪声检索条件下实现了一致的Exact Match和F1分数提升。理论分析显示平滑近似具有可控误差界，且软聚合的梯度对齐比硬选择更接近oracle目标。

Conclusion: RIMS框架有效解决了小型语言模型在检索增强生成中对噪声敏感的问题，通过软聚合机制充分利用偏好信号，在资源受限场景中实现了更好的性能，为小型模型优化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Small-scale language models (SLMs) are attractive for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) in resource-constrained settings, but their limited capacity makes them highly sensitive to noisy or spurious retrieved evidence. Existing preference-based methods such as RoseRAG select only the hardest single preference pair via hard argmin/argmax, discarding the remaining signal; others treat multiple pairs as independent binary comparisons, resulting in low data utilization. We propose RIMS, a three-stage preference optimization framework comprising (1) synthetic chain-of-thought preference data generation via rejection sampling using the target SLM itself without relying on proprietary models, (2) a differentiable soft aggregation mechanism that replaces hard selection with a smooth operator, preserving gradient signal from all preference pairs while retaining the discriminative structure of margin-aware selection, and (3) preference optimization with the smoothed objective applied to multiple alignment algorithms. We theoretically show that the smoothed approximation admits a controllable error bound and that smooth aggregation yields provably tighter gradient alignment to the oracle objective than hard selection. Experiments on four multi-hop question answering benchmarks show that our approach outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across multiple SLM backbones, achieving consistent gains in Exact Match and F1 under noisy retrieval conditions. Our implementation is available at https://github.com/tptrix29/RIMS.

</details>


### [88] [Committed Before Reasoning: Behavioral Reproduction and Preliminary Activation-Level Evidence of Answer Pre-Commitment in an Open-Weight LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16451)
*Heejin Jo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究揭示大语言模型存在"先承诺后推理"的反向推理模式：在汽车清洗任务中，模型倾向于先选择"步行"答案，然后再生成支持该选择的推理，即使该答案与任务前提矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察到聊天模型有时会先确定答案，然后生成支持该答案的推理，而不是通过推理推导出答案。这种现象即使在答案与任务前提相矛盾时也会发生。研究者希望通过一个最小化探针任务来研究这种反向推理行为。

Method: 采用实验方法：(1)行为复现：使用Qwen3-8B模型在不同系统提示条件下进行实验，分析错误承诺的发生率；(2)激活水平分析：使用预训练的无训练激活预言机在答案文本输出前探测隐藏状态；(3)方法论研究：比较开放式和封闭式问题对模型行为的影响。

Result: 实验结果显示：(1)在85-100%的采样轮次中发生错误承诺，即使在思考模式下4096个token的思考预算也无法修复；(2)激活预言机显示，在答案输出前"步行"读取显著超过中性上下文基线，即使最终选择"开车"的轮次在承诺前也显示步行倾向；(3)问题表述形式显著影响模型行为，封闭式问题使正对照从2/16提高到11/16。

Conclusion: 该研究证明大语言模型确实存在先承诺后推理的反向推理模式，这种模式即使在答案矛盾任务前提时也会发生。激活水平分析提供了初步证据表明模型在答案输出前已倾向特定选择。方法论上强调正对照在解释负预言机结果中的重要性。

Abstract: Chat models sometimes commit to an answer and then produce reasoning that justifies it rather than deriving it -- even when the answer contradicts a task premise. We study a minimal probe: "I want to wash my car. The car wash is 100 meters away. Should I walk or drive?" Only drive works (the car must be at the car wash), yet models overwhelmingly recommend walking. (1) Behavioral reproduction: on Qwen3-8B across five system-prompt conditions (210 rollouts), the wrong commitment occurs in 85-100% of sampled rollouts per condition and 100% of greedy rollouts, in both thinking and non-thinking modes; a 4,096-token thinking budget does not repair it. (2) Preliminary activation-level evidence: probing hidden states with a pretrained, training-free activation oracle (no task-specific probe training) at positions before the answer text is emitted, "walk" read-outs exceed a neutral-context baseline (68% vs. 17%; walk-committing rollouts p=.005, drive-committing rollouts p=.005, Fisher exact) -- notably, rollouts that eventually answer drive also read as walk-leaning before commitment (5/6). The oracle's default on unrelated content is "drive" (83%), so the read-outs are not lexical bias; stratifying by literal walk/drive occurrence shows they are not text recovery either (spans containing "drive" still read out walk; in balanced lexical fields, per-rollout walk-majorities beat a per-prompt neutral baseline 15/22 vs. 1/8, p=.01; drive-committing rollouts 6/6, p=.002). Samples are small and the within-rollout positional gradient is not significant (p=.34); we frame these results as preliminary. (3) Methodological: with fixed oracle, activations, and positions, question wording alone moves a positive control from 2/16 (open question) to 11/16 (closed); negative oracle results are uninterpretable without per-wording positive controls.

</details>


### [89] [Encoding EEG Signals to Examine Human-Like Next-Word Prediction Behaviour in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16549)
*Boi Mai Quach,Binh T. Nguyen,Cathal Gurrin,Graham Healy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探索语言模型（LMs）的预测能力与人类阅读认知神经信号之间的关系，通过比较人类和语言模型基于top-1预测和surprisal信息度量生成的回归因子，预测脑电图（EEG）记录的事件相关电位（ERP），发现只有surprisal与语言处理ERP相关，且模型规模扩大不一定能更好地模拟人类语言认知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型擅长预测序列中的下一个词，人类阅读也表现出类似的预测性。神经科学研究表明，下一词可预测性会影响大脑反应（通过EEG记录）。虽然高级LMs在下一词预测任务上的准确率接近人类表现，但这引出一个问题：更高的预测准确率是否意味着这些模型充分捕捉了与人类阅读理解相关的认知信号？

Method: 基于两个信息度量（top-1预测和surprisal）为人类和语言模型生成回归因子，预测来自EEG记录的事件相关电位（ERP），这些ERP反映了阅读过程中不同阶段的认知处理。通过建模ERP模式来对各种LMs在阅读过程中的认知合理性进行细粒度分析。

Result: 只有surprisal与语言处理ERP存在潜在相关性，特别是对于具有高语义内容的开放类词汇。研究结果挑战了一个假设：通过增加参数和计算预算来扩展语言模型，会持续提高与人类类似语言处理的收敛性。

Conclusion: 建模ERP模式为评估语言模型在阅读过程中的认知合理性提供了细粒度分析方法。仅凭预测准确性不能充分反映模型捕捉人类认知信号的能力，surprisal是更相关的度量。模型规模的扩大并不一定能更好地模拟人类语言认知处理，这为语言模型的认知评估提供了新视角。

Abstract: Language models (LMs) are trained to excel at predicting the next word in the sequence given prior context, and humans also share this predictability in reading comprehension. Neuroscience research reveals that next-word predictability influences brain response, as recorded at millisecond resolution using electroencephalography (EEG). While our evidence indicates that advanced LMs achieve accuracies closely aligned with human performance at the next-word prediction task, this raises the question: Does higher prediction accuracy necessarily mean that these models adequately capture the cognitive signals associated with human reading comprehension? Here, we generate regressors for both humans and LMs based on two information measures, including top-1 prediction and surprisal, to predict event-related potential (ERP) elicited from EEG recordings which reflect different stages of cognitive processing during reading. We argue that modelling ERP patterns offers fine-grained analysis of the cognitive plausibility of various LMs during reading. Our results indicate that only surprisal potentially correlates with language-processing ERPs, especially for open-class words with high semantic content. Moreover, our findings challenge the assumption that scaling LMs with increased parameters and computational budgets will consistently lead to improved convergence with human-like linguistic processing.

</details>


### [90] [NOWJ@COLIEE 2026: Adaptive Pipelines for Legal Retrieval and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16603)
*Thuong-Hieu Ngo,Hoang-Trung Nguyen,Huu-Dong Nguyen,Xuan-Bach Le,Le-Dung Nguyen,Quang-Thanh Tran,Ha-Thanh Nguyen,Thi-Hai-Yen Vuong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: NOWJ团队在COLIEE 2026竞赛中针对五个任务提出了多种创新方法，包括多阶段检索流水线、混合检索重排序、检索增强生成、动态路由管道和层次化transformer结合图推理


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参加COLIEE 2026竞赛的五个任务（Task 1-4和Pilot Task），为法律信息处理中的关键挑战（包括案例检索、蕴含关系判断、法规检索与蕴含、法律文本蕴含和判决预测）提供有效的解决方案

Method: 针对不同任务设计了专门的方法：1) Task 1使用四阶段流水线（候选过滤、稠密检索、交叉编码器重排序、自适应cutoff预测）；2) Task 2结合BM25过滤、T5重排序和LLM蕴含验证；3) Task 3采用检索增强生成框架；4) Task 4引入动态路由管道；5) Pilot Task使用层次化transformer与CRF层、参数关系挖掘和图推理

Result: 提出了针对COLIEE 2026五个任务的系统化解决方案，每项任务都设计了专门的处理流水线和方法论，展示了在法律AI任务中的全面能力

Conclusion: 通过多种创新方法在COLIEE 2026竞赛中展示了法律AI任务的综合解决方案，为法律信息检索、蕴含判断和判决预测提供了有效的技术框架

Abstract: This paper presents the methodologies and results of the NOWJ team's participation across all five tasks of the COLIEE 2026 competition. For Task 1 (Legal Case Retrieval), we propose a four-stage pipeline comprising candidate filtering, dense retrieval with complementary embedding models, cross-encoder reranking via fine-tuned generative rerankers and MLP-based pairwise classification, and adaptive per-query cutoff prediction. For Task 2 (Legal Case Entailment), we combine BM25 filtering, T5-based reranking, and LLM-based entailment verification with consensus ensemble. For Task 3 (Statute Law Retrieval and Entailment), we adopt a retrieval-augmented generation framework with dense retrieval, attention-based reranking, and few-shot-prompted LLM reasoning. For Task 4 (Legal Textual Entailment), we introduce a dynamic routing pipeline that classifies query difficulty and dispatches cases to either a balanced few-shot solver or a structured zero-shot chain-of-thought solver. For the Pilot Task (Legal Judgment Prediction), we combine hierarchical transformers with CRF layers, argument relation mining, and probabilistic argumentation graph reasoning.

</details>


### [91] [From Memory to Skills: Evidence-Grounded Co-Evolution Governance for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16621)
*Bo Tang,Yang Zhang,Guomian Zhuang,Wenqiang Wei,Gaoyang Zheng,Lindong Xie,Yanchao Tan,Feiyu Xiong,Qingyu Yang,Edward Chung,Zhiyu li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MSCE是一个无需训练的记忆-技能协同进化框架，将智能体经验组织为可执行技能和认知模型，通过反射加权价值回溯提升长期任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的长视野LLM智能体记忆系统通常将历史轨迹作为被动上下文检索，而不是将其转化为可执行的能力。这限制了智能体从经验中学习和进化的效率。

Method: MSCE框架将智能体经验组织为：1) 基础的步骤轨迹，2) 可重用的程序性策略，3) 声明性的环境认知。它通过证据支持的L2策略（具有正估计增益）固化可调用技能，这些技能保留证据链接、适用边界、决策指导、验证规则和可靠性估计。框架引入反射加权价值回溯，将稀疏的终端反馈通过密集的本地自我反思传播，产生证据校准的轨迹价值用于管理记忆和技能的进化。

Result: 在EvoAgentBench和LoCoMo基准测试中，MSCE显著优于最先进的技能增强和记忆驱动智能体基线，表现出强大的跨领域可迁移性和终身进化能力。

Conclusion: MSCE框架通过将经验转化为结构化、可执行的技能和认知模型，实现了记忆与技能的协同进化，为长视野LLM智能体提供了有效的终身学习机制，在多个基准测试中显示出优越性能。

Abstract: Existing memory systems for long-horizon LLM agents often retrieve prior traces as passive context rather than converting them into executable capabilities. In this paper, we propose MSCE, a training-free Memory--Skill Co-Evolution framework that organizes agent experience into grounded step traces, reusable procedural policies, and declarative environmental cognition. MSCE crystallizes evidence-backed L2 policies with positive estimated gain into callable skills that retain evidence links, applicability boundaries, decision guidance, verification rules, and reliability estimates. It further introduces reflection-weighted value backfilling, which propagates sparse terminal feedback through dense local self-reflections to produce evidence-calibrated trace values for governing memory and skill evolution. Experiments on EvoAgentBench and LoCoMo demonstrate that MSCE significantly outperforms state-of-the-art skill-augmented and memory-driven agent baselines, exhibiting strong cross-domain transferability and lifelong-evolution capabilities.

</details>


### [92] [OpenLanguageModel: Readable and Composable Small-Language-Model Pretraining for Education and Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16669)
*Tavish Mankash,Vardhaman Kalloli,Keshava Prasad,Deepan Muthirayan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: OpenLanguageModel (OLM) 是一个开源PyTorch库，用于构建和预训练小型语言模型，通过声明式API使模型架构完全透明，支持从教学到生产级训练的全流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的语言模型开发通常隐藏了架构实现的细节，使得从教学理解到实际训练之间存在鸿沟。研究者需要透明的模型架构来更好地理解、修改和实验，同时希望能在教学环境和生产环境中使用相同的代码。

Method: 设计了一个声明式API，其中Block、Residual、Repeat和Parallel等原语描述组件的连接方式，使模型代码与架构图对应。提供完整的工具链包括分词器、数据集、优化器、混合精度训练、回调系统和硬件感知的执行引擎。支持CPU、单GPU和单节点多GPU训练。

Result: 实现了27个预设模型覆盖9个常见模型家族。验证显示与参考实现高度一致，348M参数工作负载在4-GPU上达到90.6%的弱扩展效率。支持紧凑的架构编辑，早期可用性评估结果积极。

Conclusion: OLM通过提供完全透明的模型实现，弥合了语言模型教学与研究开发之间的差距。其声明式API使架构修改直观，完整的基础设施支持从原型到训练的平滑过渡。作为MIT许可的开源项目，OLM有望成为小型语言模型研究和教育的实用工具。

Abstract: OpenLanguageModel (OLM) is an open-source PyTorch library for building and pretraining small language models while keeping their machinery visible. In OLM, model code reads like the architecture: components are ordinary modules, while Block, Residual, Repeat, and Parallel describe how they are wired. The resulting model can move unchanged from a teaching notebook to a complete pretraining run or a research ablation. OLM connects this readable model layer to tokenizers, local and streaming datasets, optimization, mixed precision, callbacks, checkpoints, and hardware-aware CPU, single-GPU, and single-node multi-GPU execution. We demonstrate the full path by tracing GPT-2 from diagram to code, launching a FineWeb-Edu training script, replacing one attention component, and letting AutoTrainer configure the available machine. The package includes 27 presets across nine familiar model families and documentation that progresses from LM fundamentals to architecture research. Validation shows close agreement with independent reference implementations, 90.6% four-GPU weak-scaling efficiency for a 348M-parameter workload, compact architecture edits, and positive early usability results. OLM is MIT-licensed and available through PyPI, GitHub, and its documentation site.

</details>


### [93] [SpecLA: Efficient Speculative Decoding for Linear-Attention Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16673)
*Zhibin Wang,Xuying Han,Zhaohua Yang,Fuliang Liu,Xue Li,Rong Gu,Sheng Zhong,Chen Tian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SpecLA是为状态线性注意力模型设计的推测解码运行时系统，通过验证链和树结构、存储验证过程中产生的紧凑因子以及使用置信度剪枝和目标对齐的起草器，实现了1.70倍的自回归解码加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力模型虽然用循环状态替代了增长的KV缓存，但自回归解码仍然需要逐token读取、更新和写入这些状态。现有的推测解码系统是为Transformer KV缓存设计的，对于状态线性注意力模型存在三个关键问题：验证必须遵循链和分支的循环依赖关系；接受必须只更新被接受的状态轨迹；起草器需要避免提交浪费状态验证工作的候选。

Method: 提出了SpecLA系统，包含三个核心组件：1) 使用拓扑感知核验证链和树结构；2) 存储验证过程中产生的紧凑因子以恢复被接受的状态；3) 使用置信度剪枝和目标对齐的EAGLE风格起草器向验证器提供有用的候选。

Result: 在NVIDIA H100上使用公开的GDN-1.3B目标模型进行测试，SpecLA实现了最高1.70倍的端到端加速比（相比于自回归解码）。

Conclusion: SpecLA成功解决了状态线性注意力模型中推测解码的特殊挑战，通过专门设计的验证机制、状态恢复方法和起草器策略，显著提升了推理速度，为线性注意力模型的高效解码提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Linear-attention models replace the growing KV cache with recurrent states, but autoregressive decoding still reads, updates, and writes these states one token at a time. Speculative decoding can reduce this cost by verifying several draft tokens in one target pass, yet existing speculative systems are designed for Transformer KV caches. For stateful linear-attention targets, verification must follow recurrent dependencies across chains and branches, acceptance must update only the accepted state trajectory, and the drafter must avoid submitting candidates that waste stateful verification work. This paper presents SpecLA, a speculative decoding runtime for stateful linear-attention models. SpecLA verifies chains and trees with topology-aware kernels, stores compact factors produced during verification to recover accepted states, and uses confidence pruning plus a target-aligned EAGLE-style drafter to feed useful candidates to the verifier. On an NVIDIA H100 with a public GDN-1.3B target, SpecLA achieves up to 1.70x end-to-end speedup over autoregressive decoding.

</details>


### [94] [Are Arithmetic Heuristic Neurons Form-Invariant? A Mechanistic Analysis of Symbols, Text, and Code in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16693)
*Sharath Naganna,Tanvir Ahmed Sijan,Uddipta Kalita*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究表明，LLMs中的算术计算在不同格式（符号算术、自然语言问题、Python代码）中共享相同的神经元电路，失败预测源于激活状态而非不同电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在一个问题的等效表达上表现不一致，不清楚这种失败是由于不同内部电路还是共享电路的不同激活状态导致的。需要探究算术计算在不同表达格式中是否具有形式不变性。

Method: 研究采用两阶段管道：结合归因修补和激活修补，在三个Llama-3模型中识别算术启发式神经元。比较符号算术、自然语言问题和Python代码三种格式，识别共享神经元并进行针对性干预。

Result: 发现紧凑的神经元集合在所有三种格式中共享；共享电路对于后期算术计算既必要又充分；将共享神经元的激活从成功执行的一个格式转移到失败的另一个格式中，能够恢复大多数错误预测（加减法超过97%）。共享神经元在不同格式中始终属于相同的启发式家族。

Conclusion: LLMs中的算术计算在不同表达格式中具有神经元级别的形式不变性，跨格式失败源于激活状态差异而非不同电路，这支持了算术计算作为"启发式袋"由稀疏MLP神经元编码的观点。

Abstract: Large language models often succeed on one formulation of a problem while failing on an equivalent formulation. Whether these failures arise from distinct internal circuits or different activation states of a shared circuit remains unknown. Recent mechanistic interpretability studies suggest that arithmetic in LLMs emerges from a "bag of heuristics," encoded by a sparse set of MLP neurons that represent distinct arithmetic strategies. We investigate whether arithmetic heuristic neurons are form-invariant across symbolic arithmetic, natural language word problems, and Python code in three Llama-3 models. In each format, we identify arithmetic heuristic neurons using a two-stage pipeline combining attribution patching and activation patching. A compact set of neurons is shared across all three formats, and targeted interventions show this shared circuit is both necessary and sufficient for late-layer arithmetic computation. Transferring the shared neurons' activations from a successful execution in one format to a failed execution in another recovers most incorrect predictions, exceeding 97% for addition and subtraction, indicating that cross-format failures arise from activation states rather than distinct circuits. Moreover, shared neurons consistently belong to the same heuristic families across formats, demonstrating that arithmetic computation in LLMs is largely form-invariant at the neuron level.

</details>


### [95] [Though Language Models Err While They Strive: Conformal Prediction for Self-Correcting Scientific Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16704)
*Mingqiao Mo,Yunlong Tan,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SFC是一个图结构的共形预测框架，通过在生成过程中进行渐进式的绝对-连贯-事实性验证，为科学推理提供统计保证，显著减少科学原则违反并提高准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在生成技术内容时经常违反基本科学原则，这在科学应用中削弱了它们的可靠性。现有方法通常独立处理声明，未能捕捉科学推理中的逻辑依赖性，导致早期科学错误会污染后续推理步骤的级联效应。

Method: SFC将科学推理分解为原子级的绝对-连贯-事实性单元，要求每个单元既符合物理定律，又能从先前上下文中获得逻辑证实。该方法将逻辑依赖性建模为近似可推演图，通过实时验证和动态分支机制运作，当检测到科学违规时，系统使用已验证上下文作为基础分支到替代生成路径。

Result: 在PhyX多模态物理、MATH、ScienceQA和ARC Challenge等科学推理基准测试中，SFC达到50.1%的PhyX物理推理准确率，显著优于DeepSeek-R1（49.8%）和GPT-4（45.8%）。在α=0.10置信水平下，提供91.7%的科学有效性并有正式共形覆盖保证，跨多个模型架构将科学定律违反减少了73%。

Conclusion: SFC通过其图结构共形预测框架和渐进验证机制，有效解决了科学推理中的级联错误问题，为语言模型的科学应用提供了可靠的统计保证，显著提高了生成内容的科学有效性。

Abstract: Large language models frequently violate fundamental scientific principles when generating technical content, undermining their reliability in scientific applications. We introduce Scientific Feasibility Control SFC, a graph-structured conformal prediction framework that provides statistical guarantees for scientific reasoning validity through progressive absolute-coherent-factuality validation. Our approach decomposes scientific reasoning into atomic absolute-coherent-factuality units requiring both individual correctness against physical laws and logical substantiation from preceding context, addressing the cascade effect where early scientific errors contaminate subsequent reasoning steps. Unlike independence-based methods that treat claims in isolation, SFC models logical dependencies as approximate deducibility graphs and operates through real-time validation with dynamic branching when scientific violations are detected, the system branches to alternative generation paths using verified context as foundation. We demonstrate SFC across established scientific reasoning benchmarks including PhyX multimodal physics, MATH, ScienceQA, and ARC Challenge, achieving 50.1 percent accuracy on PhyX physics reasoning, substantially outperforming recent reasoning models including DeepSeek-R1 49.8 percent and GPT-4 45.8 percent while providing 91.7 percent scientific validity with formal conformal coverage guarantees at alpha equals 0.10 confidence level and reducing scientific law violations by 73 percent across multiple model architectures.

</details>


### [96] [JOR-Bench: Japanese Operations Research Benchmarks for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16777)
*Yuu Jinnai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: JOR-Bench是一个包含5个日语基准的数据集，用于评估大语言模型在运筹学问题建模和解决方面的能力，覆盖1319个问题，支持多语言模型比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运筹学基准主要是英语的，缺乏日语语言的评估工具，限制了日语环境下LLMs能力的评估和多语言比较。

Method: 将5个现有英语基准（IndustryOR, MAMO Complex LP, NL4OPT, OptiBench, OptMATH）翻译成日语，构建包含1319个问题的JOR-Bench数据集，使用Python接口到OR-Tools进行标准化执行，评估7个LLMs在英语和日语版本上的表现。

Result: 强大多语言模型的运筹学问题建模能力基本上是语言无关的，英语和日语平均准确率差异仅为-0.3个百分点。但错误分析揭示了一些跨语言差异，包括在某些领域中存在语用消歧失败，导致日语提示下模型输出决策变量值而非目标值。

Conclusion: JOR-Bench填补了日语运筹学基准的空白，为评估LLMs在日语环境下的运筹学能力提供了标准化工具，发现强大多语言模型在运筹学问题建模方面具有语言无关性，但也识别出特定的跨语言性能差异。

Abstract: We present JOR-Bench, a collection of five Japanese-language benchmarks for evaluating the ability of large language models (LLMs) to formulate and solve operations research (OR) problems. Each benchmark is a Japanese translation of an existing English benchmark: IndustryOR, MAMO Complex LP, NL4OPT, OptiBench, and OptMATH, covering 1,319 problems spanning linear programming, mixed-integer programming, non-linear programming, and combinatorial optimization. JOR-Bench is a solver-independent benchmark that can be used with any solver or programming language, and consists of pairs of Japanese problem statements and expected numerical answers. We evaluate seven LLMs, including multilingual general-purpose models and Japanese-specialized models, on both the original English and the new Japanese versions, and compare performance across languages. For the main evaluation, we standardize execution with the Python interface to OR-Tools to make model outputs comparable and reproducible with open-source software. Our results show that OR formulation ability is largely language-neutral for strong multilingual models; the overall average accuracy difference between English and Japanese is only $-0.3$ pp. Yet error analysis reveals subtle cross-lingual differences, including a pragmatic disambiguation failure in some domains that causes models to output decision-variable values instead of the objective value when the prompt is in Japanese.

</details>


### [97] [Cascading versus Joint Modeling for Hierarchical Offensive Language Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16790)
*Ruixing Ren,Junhui Zhao,Xiaoke Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了细粒度冒犯性语言检测中的级联分解与联合多任务建模两种范式，提出了一个三级级联检测系统，并通过对比实验揭示了精度与部署成本之间的量化权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对级联分解和联合多任务建模这两种建模范式的直接、可控比较，特别是从准确性、参数数量和推理延迟等维度进行系统性评估。同时，很少验证所选择的类别不平衡处理策略是否真正最优。

Method: 提出了一个三级级联检测系统，为每个子任务定制训练策略，并设计两种验证机制：1）通过受控消融研究确定每个子任务的最佳类别不平衡处理策略；2）训练一个共享编码器的联合多任务模型作为架构控制，从准确性、参数数量和推理延迟三个维度进行实际测量。

Result: 级联系统在官方测试集的三个子任务上分别获得了0.795、0.716和0.557的宏观F1分数。消融研究表明，仅凭直觉配置损失函数是次优的；基于消融结果重新配置可以提高性能和稳定性。级联架构在三个子任务上的准确性均高于联合多任务模型，在最不平衡的子任务上获得了7.1分的宏观F1增益，代价是3倍的参数和1.67倍的推理延迟。

Conclusion: 研究结果确立了级联架构在准确性优势与其部署成本之间的明确、可量化权衡。级联错误分析显示，约五分之一的错误来自第一阶段过滤器且无法被后续阶段纠正，为系统设计提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Fine-grained offensive language detection organizes labels into a hierarchical structure, for which two modeling paradigms exist: cascaded decomposition and joint multi-task modeling. Prior work rarely provides a direct, controlled comparison of the two paradigms in terms of accuracy, parameter count, and inference latency, and rarely verifies whether a chosen class-imbalance handling strategy is actually optimal. This paper proposes a three-level cascaded detection system whose training strategy is customized per subtask, together with two verification mechanisms. First, a controlled ablation study determines the best class-imbalance handling strategy for each subtask. Second, a joint multi-task model with a shared encoder is trained as an architectural control, yielding real measurements along the dimensions of accuracy, parameter count, and inference latency. Experiments show that the cascaded system attains macro-F1 scores of 0.795, 0.716, and 0.557 on the three subtasks of the official test set. The ablation study reveals that configuring the loss function purely by imbalance-severity intuition is suboptimal; reconfiguring based on the ablation results improves both performance and stability. End-to-end cascade evaluation shows that roughly one-fifth of the errors in the cascade pipeline originate from the first-stage filter and cannot be corrected by subsequent stages. Relative to the joint multi-task model, the cascaded architecture achieves higher accuracy on all three subtasks, with a 7.1-point macro-F1 gain on the most severely imbalanced subtask, at the cost of three times the parameters and 1.67 times the inference latency. Together, these results establish an explicit, quantifiable trade-off between the accuracy advantage of cascaded architectures and their deployment cost.

</details>


### [98] [Diagnosing Correctness Probes under Self-Judgement Confounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16799)
*Yi-Long Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究表明，在语言模型中，隐藏状态读出的可转移信号主要反映模型自我判断而非客观正确性语义，即使在客观正确性与自我判断相冲突的情况下也是如此。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐藏状态读出可用于预测语言模型输出是否正确，但客观正确性(OC)通常与模型自我判断(SJ)一致，这使得解码信号在语义上存在歧义。研究者希望探究当OC和SJ预测相反的读出排序时，可转移信号到底反映哪种语义。

Method: 构建OC和SJ相冲突的情况，在高置信度分歧案例上，估计与SJ和OC相关的因子方向。在四个最大14B参数的指令微调模型上进行实验，评估这些方向的极性在数学推理和事实回忆任务中的表现。研究可转移性在跨层、跨领域(包括MMLU和TruthfulQA)的稳定性，并进行答案似然性、序列长度和零方向控制。

Result: 在所有四个模型中，SJ相关方向在两个跨领域方向上均以高于随机水平转移，而OC相关方向在所有对应条件下的OC排序点估计均低于随机水平。这种转移不对称性在中后期层发展，在各种控制条件下持续存在，并扩展到MMLU和二元TruthfulQA任务中，无需目标域方向拟合。在研究的所有模型和诊断子集中，最可靠的可转移组件保持了SJ相关极性。

Conclusion: 隐藏状态读出的可转移性本身不能确立客观正确性语义。最可靠的可转移信号反映了模型的自我判断偏好，而非客观正确性。这对使用隐藏状态读出作为模型正确性预测器提出了重要警告。

Abstract: Hidden-state readouts can predict whether language-model outputs are correct, but objective correctness (OC) usually agrees with the model's own self-judgement (SJ), leaving the decoded signal semantically ambiguous. We construct conflict cases in which OC and SJ predict opposite readout orderings. On high-confidence disagreements, conventional correctness-labelled contrasts often rank incorrect/self-endorsed responses above correct/self-rejected responses, following SJ rather than OC. We estimate factorial SJ- and OC-associated directions and evaluate their polarity across mathematical reasoning and factual recall. Across four instruction-tuned models up to 14B parameters, the SJ-associated direction transfers above chance in both cross-domain directions for every model, whereas the OC-associated direction has a below-chance point estimate for the expected OC ordering in every corresponding condition. This transfer asymmetry develops across middle-to-late layers, persists under answer-likelihood, sequence-length, and null-direction controls, and extends to MMLU and binary TruthfulQA without target-domain direction fitting. Across the studied models and diagnostic subsets, the most reliably transferable component preserves SJ-associated polarity. Transferability alone therefore does not establish objective-correctness semantics.

</details>


### [99] [Schema-Constrained Document-Level Event Argument Extraction with Lightweight LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16808)
*Roberto Pietrantuono,Antonio Guerriero,Pouya Sattari*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 中等规模开源大语言模型通过角色集注入提示、参数高效微调(LoRA)和确定性解码后处理，在文档级事件论元提取任务中超越了之前的GPT基线，在MAVEN-ARG数据集上取得了最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文档级事件论元提取面临三个主要挑战：触发词与论元之间的长距离依赖关系、跨句子上下文理解、以及严格模式约束。这些挑战导致边界错误、角色不确定性和与受限模式不一致等问题。

Method: 研究方法包括：(1) 在提示中注入角色集以确保模式合规性；(2) 使用相同的JSON接口进行参数高效的监督微调（LoRA）；(3) 确定性解码结合后处理，包括验证JSON、过滤无效角色、去重论元并将跨度对齐到文档窗口。

Result: 在官方MAVEN-ARG评估器下，微调后的中等规模开源模型在提及级、实体共指和事件共指评估中均优于之前报告的GPT基线。最佳模型(Phi-4, 14B)在事件共指级别达到了42.39%的F1分数。

Conclusion: 研究表明中等规模开源大语言模型能够可靠地执行模式约束的文档级事件论元提取，通过精心设计的提示工程、参数高效微调和后处理技术，可以在计算资源有限的情况下实现高性能的事件论元提取。

Abstract: Event Argument Extraction (EAE) converts documents into structured event records by identifying argument spans and assigning them schema-defined roles. Document-level EAE is challenging due to long-range dependencies between triggers and arguments, cross-sentence context, and strict role constraints, which often lead to boundary errors, uncertainty in roles, and inconsistencies with restricted schemas.
  In this paper, we study whether mid-sized open LLMs can perform schema-constrained EAE reliably at the document level on MAVEN-ARG. Our approach combines (i) role-set injection in prompts for schema compliance, (ii) parameter-efficient supervised fine-tuning (LoRA) using the same JSON-only interface used at inference, and (iii) deterministic decoding with post-processing that validates JSON, filters invalid roles, de-duplicates arguments, and aligns spans to the document window. Under the official MAVEN-ARG evaluator, fine-tuned mid-sized open models outperform previously reported GPT baselines across mention, entity-coreference, and event-coreference evaluations; our best model (Phi-4, 14B) reaches 42.39\% F1 at the event-coreference level. Code to reproduce experiments is publicly available at https://github.com/dessertlab/EAE/.

</details>


### [100] [Group Entropy-Controlled Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16850)
*Guangran Cheng,Chengqi Lyu,Songyang Gao,Wenwei Zhang,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GEPO（组熵控制策略优化）是GRPO的轻量级扩展，通过组熵估计和执行熵条件化非对称优势塑造，解决了异构任务混合训练中全局熵调控不足和优势信号不可比较的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大型语言模型强化学习对齐过程中，异构任务混合训练会导致不同的熵制度，使得全局或标记级熵调控无法满足异构探索需求。这种异质性还使得GRPO风格的归一化优势产生熵依赖偏差，导致跨提示组的优势信号统计上不可比较。

Method: GEPO利用现有分组样本估计组熵，执行熵条件化非对称优势塑造：在低熵组衰减正优势以减少过度利用，在高熵组衰减负优势以保留探索，使用历史熵统计得出的自适应阈值。

Result: 在两个基础模型和十三个涵盖数学、物理、科学、代码生成和指令遵循的基准测试上的广泛实验表明，GEPO在一致优于GRPO和最近熵控制方法的同时，提供跨任务的平衡改进，并保持训练过程中的任务特定探索水平。

Conclusion: GEPO通过组熵条件化优势塑造有效解决了异构任务强化学习中的熵调控问题，实现了更好的探索-利用平衡，为大型语言模型对齐提供了一种有效的熵控制方法。

Abstract: Entropy control has become an effective tool in reinforcement learning (RL) of large language models (LLMs), helping balance exploration-exploitation trade-off during alignment process. Such RL paradigm is often conducted on mixtures of heterogeneous tasks, which induce distinct entropy regimes under the same policy, making global or token-level entropy regulation insufficient to corresponding heterogeneous needs of exploration. This heterogeneity further makes GRPO-style normalized advantages induce an entropy-dependent bias, making advantage signals across prompt groups statistically non-comparable. To address this issue, we propose Group Entropy-Controlled Policy Optimization (GEPO), a lightweight extension to GRPO that uses group entropy, estimated from existing grouped samples to perform entropy-conditioned asymmetric advantage shaping. GEPO attenuates positive advantages in low-entropy groups to reduce over-exploitation, and negative advantages in high-entropy groups to preserve exploration, with adaptive thresholds derived from historical entropy statistics. Extensive experiments on two base models across thirteen benchmarks spanning mathematics, physics, science, code generation, and instruction following show that GEPO consistently outperforms GRPO and recent entropy-controlled methods, delivering balanced cross-task improvements while preserving task-specific exploration levels throughout training.

</details>


### [101] [Trace-Based On-Policy Distillation for Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16872)
*Haolin Ren,Ziyang Huang,Chenhao Yuan,Jun Zhao,Kang Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于迹的在线策略蒸馏（TOPD）框架，通过教师监督的方式将推理能力迁移到扩散大语言模型，避免了奖励估计的需求，显著提升了训练效率和推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型是有前景的自回归生成替代方案，但推理导向的后训练面临挑战：监督微调需要密集但常常偏离策略的掩码状态，而强化学习依赖于稀疏奖励或价值建模。需要一种无需奖励估计的有效训练方法。

Method: 提出TOPD框架：1）从目标dLLM采样在线策略扩散轨迹；2）在相应的部分去噪状态上从教师模型获取教师标记分布；3）使用标记级别的逆KL散度目标更新目标dLLM。该方法专注于形成最终响应的迹对齐标记决策。

Result: 在数学推理基准测试中，TOPD使SDAR-4B-Chat在MATH500精度上匹配其RL训练对应模型TraDo-4B-Instruct，静态评估提升+5.7，动态评估提升+4.5。相比RL训练，TOPD使用4倍少的rollout轮次，估计达到96.0倍的模型计算加速。

Conclusion: TOPD是一种有效的教师监督框架，能够将推理能力转移到扩散大语言模型，无需奖励估计，在保持密集教师监督的同时，使训练与模型自身去噪状态对齐，显著提高了训练效率和推理性能。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) are a promising alternative to autoregressive generation. However, reasoning-oriented post-training for dLLMs remains challenging. Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) for dLLMs requires dense but often off-policy masked states, while reinforcement learning (RL) relies on sparse rewards or value modeling. This paper proposes \textbf{trace-based on-policy distillation (TOPD)}, a teacher-supervised framework that transfers reasoning ability to a target dLLM without reward estimation. The key idea is to supervise a dLLM on its own denoising trajectory, focusing on the trace-aligned token decisions that form the final response. Specifically, TOPD samples on-policy diffusion trajectories from the target dLLM, obtains teacher token distributions from a teacher model on the corresponding partially denoised states, and updates the target dLLM with a token-level Reverse Kullback-Leibler (Reverse-KL) objective. This design preserves dense teacher supervision while aligning training with the model's own denoising states. On mathematical reasoning benchmarks, TOPD enables SDAR-4B-Chat to match the MATH500 accuracy of its RL-trained counterpart TraDo-4B-Instruct, with gains of +5.7 under static evaluation and +4.5 under dynamic evaluation. Compared with the RL-trained counterpart, TOPD achieves this with 4$\times$ fewer rollout rounds, corresponding to an estimated 96.0$\times$ to-accuracy model-compute speedup.

</details>


### [102] [Real-World Evaluation of an AI Agent Drafting Translational Impact Summaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16989)
*Mohammad Arvan,Amber E. Osterholt,Bailee Rue,Yuvaneswaren Ramakrishnan Sureshbabu,Krishna Riteshkumar Patel,Rebecca T. Feinstein,Bethany C. Bray,Niranjan S. Karnik*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 开发了一个人类参与循环的AI智能体，用于自动收集学者研究影响力证据并草拟影响力总结，评估显示该智能体生成的内容81.7%被接受或编辑，将每名学者的处理时间从15小时减少到14分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床与转化科学奖(CTSA)项目需要记录学者研究影响力，但手工收集每名学者的数据约需15小时，且无法扩展到整个研究队列。需要自动化工具来解决这一可扩展性问题。

Method: 构建了一个人类参与循环的AI智能体，自动汇编每位学者的证据档案并草拟转化科学效益模型(TSBM)影响力总结。在一个CTSA中心的10名职业发展学者中评估，由两名评价人员独立编码507项发现，主要指标是双方均接受或编辑的一致可用率。

Result: 智能体生成的发现中81.7%被两位评审接受或编辑；每名学者处理时间中位数14分钟（替代原15小时）；评审间一致性中等；智能体召回率接近人工搜索；证据覆盖所有TSBM领域，约1/3发现属于常规流程易忽略的非学术类别；合成准确性评分4.5/5，有用性评分4.8/5。

Conclusion: 人类参与循环的AI智能体可作为学者影响力记录的首轮起草工具，将工作人员角色从收集写作转变为审核，使队列规模的影响力报告成为可能。

Abstract: Introduction. Clinical and Translational Science Award (CTSA) programs must document their scholars' research impact, but assembling each scholar's record by hand takes staff an estimated 15 hours and does not scale to a full cohort. An artificial intelligence (AI) agent could serve as a tool to gather scholar data across platforms and disciplines.
  Methods. We built a human-in-the-loop AI agent that assembles a dossier of sourced evidence for each scholar and drafts one-sentence Translational Science Benefits Model (TSBM) impact summaries for staff review. We evaluated it in the impact-reporting workflow of one CTSA hub across 10 career-development (KL2/K12) scholars. Two evaluation staff independently coded all 507 findings as accept, edit, or reject; the primary measure was the unanimous usable rate, defined as the share both accepted or edited.
  Results. Both reviewers accepted or edited 81.7% of the agent's findings. Reviewers each spent a median of 14 minutes per scholar, replacing an estimated 15 hours of manual assembly. Inter-rater agreement was moderate (Cohen's kappa 0.43 on the usable-versus-reject decision). A profile discovery study found the agent's recall close to human search. The agent's impact evidence spanned all four TSBM domains, and about a third of the reviewed findings fell in non-scholarly categories that routine processes tend to miss. Reviewers rated synthesis accuracy 4.5 and usefulness 4.8 on a 5-point scale.
  Conclusions. A human-in-the-loop AI agent can serve as the first-pass author of a scholar's impact record, shifting staff from collecting and writing to reviewing, and making cohort-scale impact reporting feasible.

</details>


### [103] [Learning from Synthetic Data without Model Collapse in Iterative Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17043)
*Xiaonan Luo,Yue Huang,Kehan Guo,Ping He,Chuan Zou,Ting Hua,Xiangliang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出KITE框架，通过失败引导的数据生成和边界感知的不确定性筛选来解决指令调优中合成数据自我改进时的模型崩溃问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型崩溃是学习合成数据时的核心挑战：当后续代LLM在越来越大的模型生成数据比例上训练时，性能会因覆盖范围变窄和偏差累积而下降。现有工作主要研究如何限制这种退化，但在迭代模型演化中，更重要的目标是确保每个后续模型都优于前代，这需要以可操作于数据筛选的粒度诊断崩溃。

Method: 提出KITE（Knowledge-boundary Instruction Tuning via Exploration）框架，这是一个两阶段方法：1）失败引导的数据生成；2）边界感知的不确定性筛选。该方法针对指令调优中的合成数据自我改进场景，特别关注能力极化现象（强项被强化而弱项进一步退化）。

Result: 在多个数据集和多个开源LLM上的实验表明，KITE比现有的强合成数据基线方法产生更稳定的改进。

Conclusion: 模型崩溃在合成数据自我改进中表现为能力极化而非简单的均匀性能退化，KITE框架通过针对性的数据生成和筛选策略有效缓解这一问题，实现了更稳定的迭代改进。

Abstract: Model collapse is a central challenge in learning from synthetic data: as later-generation large language models (LLMs) are trained on an increasing proportion of model-generated data, performance can degrade due to narrowed coverage and accumulated bias. Existing work mainly studies how to bound this degradation. In iterative model evolution, however, the more meaningful objective is to ensure that each successive model improves over its predecessor, which requires diagnosing collapse at a granularity that is actionable for data curation. We study this problem in synthetic data self-improving for instruction tuning. We show that collapse in this setting is not simply uniform performance degradation, but can appear as a polarization of competence, where synthetic training reinforces already strong skills while further degrading weak ones. Motivated by this observation, we propose KITE (Knowledge-boundary Instruction Tuning via Exploration), a two-stage framework that combines failure-guided data generation with boundary-aware uncertainty curation. Experiments across several datasets and multiple open-source LLMs show that KITE yields more stable improvement than strong synthetic-data baselines.

</details>


### [104] [BLAD: A Historically Contextualized, Multilingual Dataset of Bangladeshi Legal Acts (1799 to 2025)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17111)
*Adib Sakhawat*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BLAD是一个包含1484个孟加拉国法律法案的数据集，涵盖了1799年至2025年期间的法案，支持多语言和时序分析，旨在填补南亚低资源民事法系的法律NLP资源空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 填补南亚低资源、民事法系司法管辖区在法律自然语言处理资源方面的持续空白。现有法律NLP数据集主要关注高资源、普通法系司法管辖区，而孟加拉国这样的民事法系国家缺乏适合NLP研究的全面、结构化法律数据集。

Method: 构建了一个数据获取和增强管道，收集了1799年至2025年的1484个立法法案。每个法案包含完整文本、结构化章节和脚注、废止状态，以及连接至当时治理政权、国家元首和现行法律框架的元数据。数据集支持英语、孟加拉语和混合语言文档的分析。

Result: 创建了BLAD数据集，包含1484个法案，跨越200多年的立法历史。数据集公开可用，采用CC BY-SA 4.0许可，可通过Kaggle平台访问。数据集支持时序和跨语言的法律分析，为低资源民事法系的法律NLP研究提供了基础资源。

Conclusion: BLAD数据集填补了南亚民事法系法律NLP资源的空白，为研究法律演变、多语言法律文本分析和法律信息检索等方向提供了重要基础。数据集的可访问性和结构化特性使其成为推动低资源司法管辖区法律AI研究的关键资源。

Abstract: We present the Bangladesh Legal Acts Dataset (BLAD), a curated collection of 1{,}484 legislative acts enacted between 1799 and 2025. Each act is represented with its full text, structured sections and footnotes, repeal status, and metadata linking it to the governing regime, head of state, and prevailing legal framework at the time of enactment.
